c).value = h\n\n    grand_missing = 0\n    grand_approval = 0\n    grand_total = 0\n\n    # Track most errors for brief\n    facility_session_errors: List[Tuple[str, str, int]] = []\n\n    r = 2\n    for sr in summary_rows:\n        ws.cell(r, 1).value = sr.facility\n        ws.cell(r, 2).value = sr.session_id\n        ws.cell(r, 3).value = sr.missing_final_counts\n        ws.cell(r, 4).value = sr.approval_gaps\n        ws.cell(r, 5).value = sr.total_errors\n\n        grand_missing += sr.missing_final_counts\n        grand_approval += sr.approval_gaps\n        grand_total += sr.total_errors\n        facility_session_errors.append((sr.facility, sr.session_id, sr.total_errors))\n\n        r += 1\n\n    # Grand total row\n    ws.cell(r, 1).value = \"Grand Total\"\n    ws.cell(r, 2).value = \"-\"\n    ws.cell(r, 3).value = grand_missing\n    ws.cell(r, 4).value = grand_approval\n    ws.cell(r, 5).value = grand_total\n\n    facility_session_errors.sort(key=lambda t: (-t[2], t[0], t[1]))\n    top_pairs = [(f, s) for f, s, _ in facility_session_errors[:2]]\n\n    return grand_missing, grand_approval, grand_total, top_pairs\n\n\ndef build_word_brief(\n    out_path: str,\n    grand_missing: int,\n    grand_approval: int,\n    grand_total: int,\n    top_pairs: List[Tuple[str, str]],\n) -> None:\n    doc = Document()\n\n    # Build a 3-6 sentence executive summary paragraph.\n    # Requirements:\n    # - define both checks in plain language\n    # - state computed totals (grand total numbers)\n    # - at least one actionable recommendation\n    # - mention at least two specific high-priority (Facility, Session ID)\n\n    # Ensure we have two pairs to mention (fallback to placeholders if none)\n    pairs_text = []\n    for facility, session_id in top_pairs[:2]:\n        pairs_text.append(f\"({facility}, {session_id})\")\n    if len(pairs_text) < 2:\n        # Provide deterministic fallback if there aren't enough error groups\n        while len(pairs_text) < 2:\n            pairs_text.append(\"(N/A, N/A)\")\n\n    paragraph = (\n        \"This cycle count variance audit applies two checks across planned bin counts and recorded count events. \"\n        \"Missing Final Count flags bins where no final count event was recorded. \"\n        \"Approval Gap flags bins where the variance between expected and counted quantities exceeded the allowed threshold and required approval. \"\n        f\"In this run, the audit identified {grand_missing} Missing Final Counts, {grand_approval} Approval Gaps, and {grand_total} Total Errors. \"\n        f\"Prioritize immediate follow-up on high-error sessions such as {pairs_text[0]} and {pairs_text[1]}, and focus first on recounting bins with missing final events and documenting approvals for out-of-threshold variances.\"\n    )\n\n    doc.add_heading(\"Cycle Count Variance Audit — Executive Brief\", level=1)\n    doc.add_paragraph(paragraph)\n\n    doc.save(out_path)\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    # Step 0: Load sources\n    plan_wb = load_workbook(PLAN_XLSX)\n    event_wb = load_workbook(EVENT_LOG_XLSX)\n    template_wb = load_workbook(TEMPLATE_XLSX)\n\n    plan_ws = plan_wb[plan_wb.sheetnames[0]]\n    event_ws = event_wb[event_wb.sheetnames[0]]\n    template_overview_ws = template_wb[\"Overview\"]\n\n    plan_headers, plan_rows = read_table_rows(plan_ws)\n\n    # Build lookup from event log\n    final_lookup = build_latest_final_lookup(event_ws)\n\n    # Create output workbook\n    out_wb = Workbook()\n\n    # Remove default sheet\n    default_ws = out_wb.active\n    out_wb.remove(default_ws)\n\n    # 1a) Overview: copy from template, as first sheet named Overview\n    overview_ws = out_wb.create_sheet(\"Overview\", 0)\n    copy_sheet_cell_by_cell(template_overview_ws, overview_ws)\n\n    # 1b) RawData: copy plan table exactly\n    raw_ws = out_wb.create_sheet(\"RawData\")\n    write_table(raw_ws, plan_headers, plan_rows)\n\n    # 1c) Formatted Data\n    formatted_ws = out_wb.create_sheet(\"Formatted Data\")\n    formatted_headers, formatted_rows = build_formatted_rows(plan_headers, plan_rows, final_lookup)\n    write_table(formatted_ws, formatted_headers, formatted_rows)\n\n    # 1d) Summary\n    summary_ws = out_wb.create_sheet(\"Summary\")\n    summary_rows =
